MA	<p>3923_d1e8</p> <p>Restriction enzymes are proteins that cut DNA at specific sequences. <span style="color: #0067cc;"><i>AfaI</i></span> is derived from Acidiphilium facilis 28H, and <span style="color: #00775f;"><i>BlnI</i></span> is derived from Brevibacterium linens. Both enzymes are labeled on the map.</p><p><strong>DNA Fragment Question:</strong> Shown below is a short DNA fragment that is only 10 kb in length.
